# Cross-arm disagreements

A is `claude-sonnet-5`; B is `claude-opus-5`. 4134 of 4134 pairs judged.

Agreement is not evidence: two arms sharing a prompt can be wrong together, and those pairs sort into `agree` unquoted. Use this to choose what to open in the gallery, not to decide what is right.

Each section starts with its icon names, comma-separated. Paste one into the gallery's “Filter by name list” box (`npm run gallery:icon-descriptions`) to see just those rows beside the artwork.

## substantial (29)

```
gunshot, tearing, forward-field, lightning-shadow, clout, abstract-117, flying-fox, stockpiles, fomorian, black-sea, convince, pick-of-destiny, abstract-120, daemon-pull, fishhook-fork, venezuela, ember-shot, lightning-shout, duck-palm, frog-foot, cowboy-holster, podium-third, test-tube-held, incoming-rocket, fire-dash, egypt, abstract-048, mute, oasis
```

### gunshot

_A depicts a gun muzzle firing flame; B depicts a bullet in flight with motion trails._

- **A** A ring-muzzled tube emitting a jagged burst of flame from its end, depicting a weapon firing.
- **B** A bullet streaking diagonally with motion trails behind it and a jagged burst of flame or impact splash flaring from its tip.

### tearing

_A depicts a sheet splitting into two torn pieces; B depicts a claw-like spike with slash marks tearing flesh_

- **A** Two jagged, torn-edged shapes pulling apart from each other like a ripped sheet of material splitting into two curved pieces.
- **B** A long tapering claw-like spike sweeping down beside two curved crescent slash marks, suggesting flesh being ripped.

### forward-field

_A depicts a castle-like structure under an arc; B depicts a spaceship under a shield_

- **A** A crescent-shaped arc curving over a spired, castle-like structure with a pointed archway and angled side towers.
- **B** A front-facing spaceship with swept wings and a pointed nose, beneath an arcing dome-like shield curving over it.

### lightning-shadow

_A shows a lightning bolt with a circular target/crosshair symbol; B shows a lightning bolt striking a crouching humanoid figure cut from the shape_

- **A** A jagged lightning bolt intersected by a circular target or crosshair symbol against a large dark curved shape.
- **B** A jagged lightning bolt striking a crouching humanoid figure whose form is cut out of a large dark mass, with further bolts splitting the shape.

### clout

_A depicts a bullet-like shape with spikes; B depicts a fist punching with radiating spikes_

- **A** A rounded bullet-like shape with sharp jagged spikes bursting outward from one end, suggesting rapid impact or explosive motion.
- **B** A clenched fist on an outstretched arm thrusting diagonally downward, backed by jagged radiating spikes conveying the force of a punch.

### abstract-117

_A describes a tram/trolley vehicle; B describes a building-like abstract block_

- **A** A front-facing tram or trolley car with a curved roofline, a windshield window, and six round wheels along its base.
- **B** A symmetrical building-like block with an arched top canopy, tall slot openings, a wide rounded panel, and a row of six circles above a base bar.

### flying-fox

_A depicts a zip-line rider on a cable; B depicts a bat hanging from a branch_

- **A** A person suspended in a harness with two wheels rolling along a diagonal cable, depicting a zip-line rider.
- **B** A bat hanging upside down from a diagonal branch, its head with round ears at the top and one wing spread below.

### stockpiles

_A depicts a pickaxe striking a mountain peak; B depicts piles of material being poured/stacked, no pickaxe or mountain._

- **A** A pickaxe striking the peak of a mountain range, sending small chip fragments flying off the summit.
- **B** Two conical heaps of loose material, with grains and chunks tumbling down from a chute or plank above onto the larger pile.

### fomorian

_A shows a figure standing by a grave/cross; B shows a giant swinging a spiked club, no grave_

- **A** A hunched humanoid figure with a bald head standing near a grave marked by a large cross, atop a hilly landscape.
- **B** A hulking humanoid giant with one oversized arm hunched on a ridge of ground, swinging a jagged club or spiked weapon overhead.

### black-sea

_A describes a jagged blob/coastline shape, B describes a wave with a foam crack_

- **A** An irregular jagged solid blob shape with pointed spiky edges resembling a coastline or torn fragment.
- **B** A dark rolling wave with a jagged white crack of foam splitting its upper surface, forming a peaked breaker.

### convince

_A depicts a single face speaking toward its own raised fist; B depicts two separate faces with one whispering into the other's ear_

- **A** A face in profile speaking toward a clenched fist raised near its chin.
- **B** Two faces in profile close together, one leaning in to speak or whisper toward the other's ear.

### pick-of-destiny

_A depicts a skull with horns; B depicts a triangular pick bearing a demon face_

- **A** A stylized tribal skull with two large spiral horns curling outward above the cranium, resembling a ram-skull design.
- **B** A triangular guitar pick decorated with a horned demon face formed by two spiral scrolls and jagged fanged markings.

### abstract-120

_A describes a meditating humanoid figure, B describes a stylized flower - entirely different subjects_

- **A** A meditating humanoid figure with a round head and body seated cross-legged with arms resting on knees.
- **B** A stylized flower: a round bud with a small knob on top set in a squared cup, above a straight stem flanked by four angled leaf-like bars and a wide base.

### daemon-pull

_A describes a neutral figure standing above wavy fabric/ground with no danger implied; B describes the figure being seized by a demonic fanged maw, a distinct threatening subject_

- **A** A humanoid figure with raised arms and a small oval head standing above a wavy, crown-like shape resembling flowing fabric or ground.
- **B** A human figure with arms flung upward being dragged down by a huge fanged maw rising from below, a depiction of a soul seized by a demon.

### fishhook-fork

_A describes claw/slash marks, B describes curved barbed fork/gaff prongs_

- **A** Three parallel diagonal streaks tapering to sharp points, resembling claw marks or slashing trails across the frame.
- **B** Three long curved barbed prongs converging diagonally, forming a multi-tined fishing hook or gaff.

### venezuela

_country map silhouette vs. elephant figure_

- **A** A solid black silhouette of a country's outline with jagged coastal edges and an inland lake shape cut out near the top.
- **B** An elephant in profile facing left, its trunk curled upward and a large ear and tusk visible.

### ember-shot

_A shows flame trailing toward a paw print; B shows a round projectile/ball with flame trail and punched holes_

- **A** A comet-like trail of jagged flame shapes streaking toward a paw print with a large round pad and smaller toe circles.
- **B** A round projectile trailing a long sweep of curling flame, with small circular holes punched around the ball.

### lightning-shout

_A depicts a plain lightning bolt shape; B depicts a shouting head emitting lightning bolts from its mouth_

- **A** A jagged lightning bolt cutting diagonally across the frame from top-left to bottom-right, the classic sign for electricity or a sudden shock.
- **B** A head shown in profile with jagged lightning bolts blasting outward from its mouth, the usual shorthand for a thunderous magical shout.

### duck-palm

_A describes a palm tree with fronds; B describes a duck foot with webbed toes_

- **A** A stylized palm tree trunk topped by wide, flowing fronds that curve outward and downward like a splayed cloak.
- **B** A webbed duck foot spread wide, with three clawed toes joined by membranes and a short leg stub at the top.

### frog-foot

_A depicts an actual webbed foot with membrane; B depicts a footprint/track, not the foot itself_

- **A** A webbed foot with four toes ending in rounded pads, joined by a curved membrane.
- **B** A four-toed footprint with rounded pads at the tip of each splayed toe, in the manner of a tree frog's clinging foot.

### cowboy-holster

_A describes an abstract bird-head-with-eye shape; B describes a realistic gun holster with visible grip, belt, and cartridge loops_

- **A** A leather holster with fringed edges shaped like a stylized bird head with a single eye at the top.
- **B** A leather revolver holster hanging from a belt with cartridge loops, the gun's grip protruding at the top, standard gear of the Old West gunslinger.

### podium-third

_A shows figure on tallest (first-place) step; B shows figure on lowest (third-place) step_

- **A** A figure with one arm raised standing atop the tallest step of a three-tiered podium, denoting a winner's ranking ceremony.
- **B** A three-tiered winners' podium with a figure standing on the lowest step raising one arm, marking a third-place finish.

### test-tube-held

_A shows an intact capped test tube being held; B shows the test tube broken in two with pieces separating and droplets flying_

- **A** A hand holding a capped test tube between two fingers, associated with science or alchemical experimentation.
- **B** A hand gripping a test tube that has snapped in two, with the broken halves separating and droplets flying off to the side.

### incoming-rocket

_A describes converging rays into a starburst/explosion with no projectile shape; B describes an actual rocket body with a leaf-shaped opening trailing exhaust streaks_

- **A** Multiple triangular rays converging diagonally toward a jagged starburst explosion in the lower corner.
- **B** A pointed projectile diving toward the lower right with a leaf-shaped opening in its body, trailing broad radiating streaks of exhaust behind it.

### fire-dash

_A depicts a lightning-bolt humanoid runner silhouetted against flame; B depicts a flame pierced by a lightning-bolt arrow shape, no humanoid figure_

- **A** A running humanoid figure formed from jagged lightning-bolt shapes, silhouetted against a large flame, suggesting extreme speed.
- **B** A billowing flame with trailing tongues, pierced by a jagged lightning-bolt arrow shape driving through its center, suggesting a burst of fiery speed.

### egypt

_A depicts a country map silhouette; B depicts the Sphinx's head/bust_

- **A** A solid angular silhouette resembling the outline of a country's map, with a notch cut into its upper right edge.
- **B** The Great Sphinx's head and shoulders in profile, wearing a nemes headdress with a thin cut marking the face, a landmark emblem of ancient Egypt.

### abstract-048

_robotic head vs abstract emblem of arcs/spike_

- **A** A round robotic head with a diamond-shaped face plate and a wide visor containing two rectangular eyes.
- **B** An abstract emblem combining a ring of arcs, a tall vertical diamond spike, and a horizontal capsule band bearing two rounded rectangles like goggle lenses.

### mute

_A depicts a silhouette with a squiggle over the mouth and a staff crossing the chest; B depicts a face with a finger held to the lips in a shushing gesture._

- **A** A silhouetted head with a wavy mustache-like mark over the mouth and a vertical staff or scepter crossing the chest.
- **B** A face with a raised index finger held vertically across the lips in a "shhh" gesture, the common sign for silence.

### oasis

_oval shape described as solid land mound (A) versus a water pool (B)_

- **A** Two curved palm trees with fanned fronds standing above a solid oval mound of land with two curved light stripes across it.
- **B** Two palm trees rising behind an oval pool marked with pale streaks suggesting ripples or reflected light.

### hollow-cat

_lying down vs seated pose_

- **A** An outlined cat lying down with a pointed-ear head, oval eyes, and a curled tail forming a loop near its body.
- **B** A seated cat in profile with pointed ears and slanted eyes, its long tail sweeping around and curling into a spiral against its body.

### triple-corn

_B mentions small oval accents between the horns; A does not_

- **A** A circular emblem formed by three curling, spiral-tipped shapes radiating from the center in a triskelion-like rotational pattern.
- **B** A circular emblem formed of three interlocking spiral-tipped horn shapes with small oval accents between them, a triskelion-like knotwork disc.

### camel

_neck lowered vs raised, and B adds a tufted tail_

- **A** A side-view silhouette of a two-humped camel standing with its head lowered on a curved neck.
- **B** A two-humped camel standing in profile with long neck raised and tufted tail.

### school-of-fish

_A shows fish repeating in the same direction; B shows a denser overlapping group swimming in alternating rows._

- **A** A repeating pattern of multiple simple fish silhouettes with round eyes and curved gill marks, swimming in the same direction.
- **B** A dense group of overlapping fish with round eyes and gill marks, swimming in rows in alternating directions.

### gaze

_minor variation in described sub-elements (side pupils vs lidded eyes with arch)_

- **A** A tall oval bisected by two vertical bars and a horizontal eye shape, containing a large central pupil and two smaller side pupils.
- **B** An oval face-like form containing a large dark ellipse above two lidded eyes with round pupils and a rounded arch below, crossed by vertical and horizontal bars.

### podium-second

_A says figure stands on the lowest platform, B says lower-left step representing second place (typically mid-height)_

- **A** A human figure with one arm raised standing on the lowest of three stepped platforms of differing heights.
- **B** A figure with one arm raised standing on the lower left step of a three-tiered winners' podium, marking a second-place finish.

### card-ace-diamonds

_placement of diamond pips: flanking A vs at upper-left/lower-right corners_

- **A** A rounded rectangular playing card showing a large letter A flanked by two diamond shapes, the ace of diamonds.
- **B** A rounded-corner playing card bearing a large letter A with a diamond pip at the upper-left and lower-right corners, the ace of diamonds.

### helmet

_B includes a neck flap detail not mentioned in A_

- **A** A rounded dome shape with a circular eye hole cut near its base, resembling a simplified helmet.
- **B** A domed helmet seen from the side with a round eye-hole ring and a neck flap sweeping down at the back.

### bullet-bill

_B mentions a curved tail fin not noted in A; A specifies a crescent-shaped single eye vs B's angry eye_

- **A** A rounded bullet-shaped projectile with a single crescent eye and jagged toothy grin along its front edge.
- **B** A bullet-shaped projectile with a curved fin at its tail, drawn with an angry eye and a wide grinning mouth full of teeth.

### coma

_B mentions a pale disc on forehead crossed by pulse line, A only mentions pulse line_

- **A** An oval head with closed drooping eyes and a downturned mouth, its forehead showing a heartbeat pulse line, suggesting unconsciousness or a coma state.
- **B** An oval head with shut curved eyes and a downturned mouth, its forehead bearing a pale disc crossed by a heartbeat trace line.

### floating-platforms

_position of the three bars relative to the rock mass (below vs above) differs_

- **A** A jagged triangular landmass with a spiky underside floating above three horizontal bars of decreasing size.
- **B** A large downward-pointing wedge of rock with a jagged underside and flat top, with three thin horizontal bars hovering above it.

### mining-helmet

_A has a bracket-shaped strap detail on top; B describes ridged crown bands and a broad brim instead._

- **A** A rounded hard hat with a front-mounted circular headlamp and a bracket-shaped strap detail on top.
- **B** A hard hat seen from the side with ridged crown bands, a broad brim, and a cylindrical lamp mounted on the front.

### banknote

_B specifies the currency letter as a struck-through G, A leaves it generic_

- **A** A rectangular paper bill with an oval centerpiece bearing a currency letter, flanked by two small dots and curved corner motifs.
- **B** A rectangular banknote with a border, two small circles, and a central oval medallion bearing a struck-through letter G currency sign.

### evil-bud

_A includes a grinning mouth (face) on the bud; B omits any face and adds thorns._

- **A** A round bulbous plant bud with a jagged grinning mouth and pointed leaf-like protrusions atop a curved stem.
- **B** A swelling flower bud on a forked, thorny stem, flanked by curling pointed leaves.

### trilobite

_antennae direction: A says antennae extend upward, B says antennae sweep back/around_

- **A** A segmented oval trilobite with ridged banded body sections and two long curling antennae extending upward from its head.
- **B** A trilobite seen from above, with a domed head shield, segmented tapering body, and two long curving antennae sweeping back around it.

### necklace-display

_B adds two small round stones flanking the pendant, not mentioned in A_

- **A** A bust silhouette showing the neck and shoulders adorned with a beaded necklace featuring a large central oval pendant.
- **B** A bust-shaped jewelry display stand wearing a wide collar necklace set with a large oval gem flanked by two small round stones.

### horned-reptile

_Eye shape (narrow vs small round) and mouth detail (fangs vs tapering snout with slit markings) differ slightly._

- **A** A reptilian creature's head with two long curved horns sweeping upward, narrow eyes, and small fangs beneath its jaw.
- **B** A frontal reptilian head with two long curved horns sweeping up and outward, small round eyes, and a narrow tapering snout with slit markings.

### nuclear-waste

_A shows all three barrels marked with the trefoil, B shows only the front barrel marked_

- **A** Three cylindrical barrels each marked with a radiation trefoil symbol, denoting hazardous nuclear waste storage.
- **B** Three barrels grouped together, the front one marked with a trefoil radiation symbol, denoting radioactive or toxic material.

### fluffy-cloud

_B adds trailing hooked wisps at the edges, not mentioned in A_

- **A** A rounded shape formed of overlapping spiral curls, resembling a stylized swirling cloud or wave.
- **B** A rounded cloud mass formed of curling spiral scrolls, with hooked wisps trailing from its edges.

### sharp-crown

_gem detail (set gems vs cutouts) and eye shape (crescent marks vs narrow leaf-shaped slits)_

- **A** A curved crown with five sharp triangular peaks each set with an oval gem, floating above two crescent-shaped eye marks, a traditional emblem of royalty.
- **B** A crown with tall sharp spikes and oval gem cutouts, above two narrow leaf-shaped eye slits suggesting a masked face beneath it.

### one-eyed

_one describes a four-pointed sparkle where the other eye would be, the other a cross-shaped scar_

- **A** An oval face shape with a single eye on one side and a four-pointed sparkle in place of the other.
- **B** A solid oval head bearing a single eye with a round pupil, beside a cross-shaped scar where the other eye would be.

### perspective-dice-six-faces-six

_whether the pips on the visible faces sum to six or each face individually shows six pips_

- **A** A cube-shaped die viewed from an angle showing three sides, each marked with pips totaling six.
- **B** A cubic die seen in three-quarter perspective, its top and two side faces each marked with six pips.

### jumping-rope

_pose of legs/arms differs (one leg raised vs arms spread downward)_

- **A** A human figure with one leg raised, arms holding a large loop that arcs overhead like a rope.
- **B** A stick figure with arms spread downward, an arc of rope swinging overhead from hand to hand.

### lightning-frequency

_B adds barbed spikes branching from bends, A describes only stacked zigzag segments_

- **A** A jagged diagonal lightning bolt composed of multiple sharp zigzag segments stacked end to end.
- **B** A jagged zigzag lightning bolt running diagonally with sharp barbed spikes branching from each bend.

### pollen-dust

_A describes a drooping flower with curled petals shedding dots below; B describes a curling stem with a bulbous bud releasing a dust cloud_

- **A** A drooping flower with curled petals shedding scattered round dots below it, representing falling pollen or dust.
- **B** A curling flower stem with petals and a bulbous bud releasing a scattered cloud of round dots, depicting pollen drifting away.

### abstract-032

_A describes two small connected circles at top/bottom, B describes pairs of ringed circles joined by bars_

- **A** A rounded rectangular shape filled with vertical stripes, bordered top and bottom by two small connected circles.
- **B** A rounded barrel-like body ribbed with vertical bars, flanked top and bottom by pairs of ringed circles joined by short connecting bars.

### hanger

_garment hanging beneath vs cloth draped over crossbar_

- **A** A triangular clothes hanger with a curved hook, holding a rectangular garment hanging beneath it.
- **B** A clothes hanger with a curved hook, draped with a rectangular cloth hanging over its crossbar.

### meat

_A describes forked bone ends and a swirl cross-section, B describes knobbed bone ends and a ring of fat_

- **A** A cylindrical cut of meat on the bone with a cross-section swirl visible and forked bone ends at both tips.
- **B** A large cut of meat on the bone, with knobbed bone ends protruding at each side and a ring of fat visible at the cut end.

### paw-front

_B includes a jagged crown-like band above the claws; A describes only the three pointed shapes without this element._

- **A** Three tall pointed shapes with hollow leaf-like centers rising side by side like flames or claws.
- **B** Three pointed leaf-shaped claw marks fanned out beneath a jagged crown-like band, suggesting the front of an animal's clawed paw.

### vine-flower

_A describes sunburst center, B describes spiked center_

- **A** A rounded five-petaled flower with a sunburst center, surrounded by curling vine-like tendrils spiraling outward in the corners.
- **B** A five-petaled blossom with a radiating spiked center, surrounded by curling tendrils and spirals suggesting vines.

### martini

_B adds a slanted swizzle stick at the rim not mentioned in A_

- **A** A triangular martini glass on a stem and base, filled with liquid, garnished with an olive on a pick.
- **B** A cone-bowled cocktail glass on a stem and flared foot, filled with liquid, with an olive and a slanted swizzle stick at the rim.

### spider-alt

_B adds eye detail (four pale eyes) not mentioned in A; leg description angular vs jointed/spread_

- **A** A stylized spider viewed from above, with a rounded teardrop-shaped abdomen, a smaller head, jagged angular legs, and small mouthparts.
- **B** A spider seen from above with a large teardrop abdomen, rounded head bearing four pale eyes, small fangs, and eight jointed legs spread outward.

### edged-shield

_emblem on shield differs: zigzag X-band vs. flared eight-armed Maltese-style cross_

- **A** A rounded shield emblazoned with a bold zigzag X-shaped band crossing its center.
- **B** A shield with a pointed base, bearing a flared eight-armed cross with concave edges, resembling a Maltese cross.

### slicing-arrow

_single arrowhead with fletching vs double-headed arrow_

- **A** An arrow with a swallowtail fletching flying diagonally amid several radiating triangular motion lines converging toward its arrowhead.
- **B** A double-headed arrow slashing diagonally, flanked by tapering motion streaks radiating from the lower-right corner to convey rapid slicing movement.

### health-decrease

_A describes an equilateral cross with chevrons cut into the vertical arm; B describes a rounded cross with triangles cut through its center_

- **A** A thick equilateral cross with three stacked downward-pointing triangular chevrons cut into its vertical arm, indicating a decrease in health.
- **B** A thick rounded cross with three stacked downward-pointing triangles cut through its center, marking falling health.

### earwig

_B mentions wing plates and radiating lines (motion/emphasis marks) not noted in A_

- **A** An elongated insect with a curved segmented body, small circular head details, and pincer-like appendages extending from the rear.
- **B** An insect seen from above with a segmented body, two pincer-like tail forceps, antennae and wing plates, with short radiating lines around it.

### oni

_A specifies narrow slanted eyes; B specifies pointed ears and glaring eyes instead_

- **A** A horned demon mask with sharp curved horns, narrow slanted eyes, and bared fangs, resembling a Japanese hannya theatrical mask.
- **B** A horned demon's face with pointed ears, glaring eyes, bared fangs and a grimacing mouth, in the manner of a Japanese oni or hannya mask.

### sword-tie

_orientation of central sword (downward vs vertical) and description of central figure (hooded/robed figure vs sword-shaped humanoid)_

- **A** Three vertical sword shapes silhouetted together, the outer two smaller and flanking a larger central sword-shaped humanoid figure.
- **B** A hooded or robed figure with a downward-pointing sword overlaid on its body, flanked by two smaller upright swords.

### dread-skull

_A describes crown-like points and crescent eyes; B describes cracks in the cranium and gapped teeth_

- **A** A stylized skull with jagged crown-like points on top, crescent-shaped eyes, and a triangular nose, evoking menace or death.
- **B** A menacing skull with angled glaring eye sockets, jagged cracks splitting the cranium, a pointed nose hole and gapped square teeth.

### lizardman

_A describes slanted eyes and cheek bumps with no crest, B describes a domed crest and round eyes; minor differences in head details_

- **A** A rounded reptilian head with narrow slanted eyes, small round cheek bumps, and a wide jagged-toothed mouth spanning the lower face.
- **B** A reptilian head with a domed crest, small round eyes and a wide grinning maw of jagged interlocking fangs.

### cube

_B describes thin gaps separating the faces, A does not mention this detail_

- **A** A three-dimensional cube shown in isometric view with visible top, left, and right faces.
- **B** A cube seen in isometric view, its top and two side faces separated by thin gaps.

### battle-axe

_B adds a squared pommel detail at haft end not mentioned in A_

- **A** A double-bladed axe head with two curved crescent blades on either end of a diagonal handle.
- **B** A double-bladed axe with crescent heads flaring from both sides of a diagonal haft that ends in a squared pommel.

### hole

_B emphasizes perspective/depth and uneven rim thickness suggesting a pit, A describes a uniform flat elliptical band_

- **A** A wide flattened oval ring forming a thin elliptical band around an empty center.
- **B** A wide ellipse ring seen in perspective, thicker along its far rim, reading as the mouth of a pit or hole in the ground.

### mecha-mask

_B adds horn-like crests not mentioned in A_

- **A** A robotic helmet or face plate with angular geometric panels, a vertical ridge down the center, and jagged side vents.
- **B** An angular robotic faceplate built from sharp wedge-shaped plates, with horn-like crests, swept side vanes and a narrow central ridge running down the brow.

### coliseum

_B specifies a jagged break dividing the facade into taller left and lower right sections, a detail A omits._

- **A** A curved multi-tiered building facade with rows of arched openings, resembling the Roman Colosseum amphitheater.
- **B** The Roman Colosseum's ruined amphitheater facade, with tiers of arched openings and a jagged break splitting the taller left section from the lower right wall.

### artillery-shell

_A places the banded stripes near the pointed tip, B places them near the flat base_

- **A** A cylindrical shell with a rounded end and diagonal banded stripes near its pointed tip, tilted at an angle.
- **B** A pointed artillery shell tilted diagonally, its casing marked with a band of angled slashes near the flat base.

### star-prominences

_overlay shape described as crescent moon vs. a disc marked with rings_

- **A** An eight-pointed star burst with a large crescent moon shape overlaid across its center, dotted with several small circular craters.
- **B** An eight-pointed spiked star burst with a large disc on it, the disc marked with rings and dots suggesting craters or spots.

### abstract-092

_B specifies exact counts and placements (two bars, two slits, two rows of three circles at corners) while A describes more generally with rows of dots_

- **A** A square filled with interlocking curved bars and rows of small dots forming an abstract geometric pattern.
- **B** A square filled with an abstract pattern of two rounded interlocking bars, two curved slits, and two rows of three small circles at opposite corners.

### hades-symbol

_B adds diamond base detail not mentioned in A_

- **A** A stylized emblem of two sharp angular prongs above a jagged zigzag band, forming an abstract two-pronged fork shape.
- **B** An angular emblem of two tall spikes rising from a broad diamond base crossed by a bold zigzag stroke, an underworld sigil.

### swordman

_lunging vs striding pose; stick-limbed detail_

- **A** A silhouetted figure lunging forward while holding a sword in one hand and a shield in the other.
- **B** A striding stick-limbed warrior figure holding a sword in one hand and a curved shield in the other.

### schrodingers-cat-alive

_A shows just head above box; B includes chest and curling tail also emerging._

- **A** A cat's head with pointed ears rising above an open box marked with a radiation symbol, referencing the Schrödinger's cat thought experiment.
- **B** A cat's head, chest and curling tail rising from a box whose flaps are folded out and whose front bears a radiation trefoil.

### spine-arrow

_A describes shaft as zigzag lightning-bolt segments, B describes shaft as lined with barbed spines/fins with a triangular head_

- **A** A jagged, segmented arrow made of angular zigzag sections pointing diagonally downward like a lightning bolt.
- **B** A jagged arrow angling down to the right, its shaft lined with barbed spines or fins and tipped by a large triangular head.

### polar-bear

_A shows bear in profile view, B shows head turned toward viewer; B mentions humped back_

- **A** A stocky bear shown in profile with a rounded body, small ears, and jagged claw-tipped paws.
- **B** A bear standing on all fours with a humped back, rounded ears and clawed paws, its head turned toward the viewer.

### empty-wood-bucket

_A places the rimmed band near the base, B places the rim at the top and a separate studded hoop near the base; B also notes a top-down view and gaps between staves._

- **A** A tapered bucket with vertical wooden slats and a rimmed rounded band near its base.
- **B** A tapered wooden bucket seen from slightly above, its staves separated by gaps, with a rim band at the top and a studded hoop near the base.

### bookmarklet

_forked ribbon tail vs plain ribbon extending past the book's edge_

- **A** An open book with a long forked ribbon marker draped down from between its pages, indicating a saved reading position.

_straight vs diagonal arrow piercing, and stem shape differs (small vs curved)_
